> **2026-10-04: the project moved from IL to C#.** The source is now `src/Companions/*.cs`, built with
> `dotnet build` (see the repo README). It was decompiled from the shipped 1.4.74 `Companions.dll`, and the
> four `MHR_*.cs` helpers are the original hand-written sources. The rebuilt DLL was checked against 1.4.74
> member by member: same signatures, calls, field accesses, strings, constants and embedded textures. The
> only differences are dead code the IL edits left behind (unreachable, so it never ran) and one fix:
> `CombatPatches.EquipBestWeapon_PreferHighestDamage` had its `[HarmonyPrefix]` on the class and
> `[HarmonyPostfix]` on the `Prefix` method (an ilasm `.custom` placement slip), so Harmony used `Prefix` as
> the postfix and never ran `Postfix`. The attributes are now on the right methods.
>
> Everything below that talks about `Companions_source.il`, ilasm/monodis, `.maxstack`, branch checks,
> the BepInPlugin byte blob or "transplanting" helpers describes the old IL workflow and no longer applies.
> The version now lives in one place in code: `CompanionsPlugin.PluginVersion` (plus `package/manifest.json`
> and `package/CHANGELOG.md`).

# Offline Companions (ProfMags) — Valheim 1.0 compatibility patch — progress notes

## IMPORTANT: verification pipeline now includes check_maxstack.py
1.4.44 shipped two new helpers with `.maxstack` too small (MHR_RadialDiag 6>5, MHR_SetBedCollisionIgnored 4>3). Unity's Mono throws `InvalidProgramException` at JIT; neither ilasm NOR the verify_all.exe JIT-prep sweep (desktop Mono 6.8) catches it - the sweep's "maxstack coverage" assumption from 1.4.31 is WRONG. New `check_maxstack.py` simulates stack depth per method (call/newobj signatures parsed, branch/try/catch aware) and flags peak > declared. First run also found two LATENT bugs from 1.4.29: `UpdateCrafting` (Cook/Homestead) and FarmController `UpdateTakingSeeds` call the 8-arg `Inventory.AddItem` -> peak 9 vs .maxstack 8 -> would crash the first time a companion crafts or takes seeds. **Always run: ilasm -> check_branches.py -> check_maxstack.py -> verify_all.exe -> BepInPlugin version check.** When writing new methods, count the stack for string.Format/object[] logging blocks - that's where these came from.

Current version: **1.4.52** — build tag `v70-despawn-popup`. Verified: branches 0/1648, maxstack 0/1648, refs 0, JIT 1595/1595, BepInPlugin 1.4.52. Untested in-game.
- User chose a Yes/No popup for despawn. `DoDespawn` now stores the target in new field `_mhrDespawnTarget`, hides the wheel, and pushes the game's `YesNoPopup(header, text, yes, no, localizeText:false, coverBackground:false)` via `UnifiedPopup.Push` (falls back to despawning directly if `UnifiedPopup.IsAvailable()` is false). Yes -> `MHR_DespawnConfirmed()` (Pop, then the old removal body: PrepareForDespawn, MHR_ReleaseBedForDespawn, DespawnWithTombstone, clear m_onDeath, ZNetView.Destroy). No -> `MHR_DespawnCancelled()` (Pop, clear target). Popups don't close themselves - both callbacks must call `UnifiedPopup.Pop()`. Localization: removed "hc_radial_despawn_confirm" default, added "hc_despawn_popup_title" ("Despawn Companion") and "hc_despawn_popup_text" ("Permanently remove {0}? ..."). `_despawnConfirmPending`/`UpdateDespawnLabel` are now dead code (harmless).

Previous version: **1.4.51** — build tag `v69-despawn-and-equip-flag-fix`. Verified: branches 0/1646, maxstack 0/1646, **refs 0 unresolved (new check_refs.py)**, JIT 1593/1593, BepInPlugin 1.4.51. Untested in-game.
- User log 77fee611 (1.4.50): Despawn threw `MissingMethodException CompanionSetup.CreateCompanionTombstone(...18 args)` - `DespawnWithTombstone` was never updated when 1.4.32 added hasHomeBed/homeBedPos (20 args). **ilasm happily emits a MemberRef to a method that doesn't exist**, and none of our checks caught it (JIT sweep only fails when the call is actually JIT-resolved... it wasn't). New `check_refs.py` compares every `call/callvirt/newobj/ldftn class Companions.X::M(...)` against defined methods (name + arg count, generic defs handled). **Add it to the pipeline: ilasm -> check_branches -> check_maxstack -> check_refs -> verify_all (run from inside verify_run) -> BepInPlugin check.** Fix: pass `false, Vector3.zero`; new `CompanionSetup._mhrDespawning` makes MHR_GetDeathItemRule return 3 (drop all) so nothing is "kept" for a companion that will never respawn; DoDespawn calls new `CompanionRest.MHR_ReleaseBedForDespawn()` (ReleaseClaimedBed -> clears bed ZDO label, HasHomeBedHash=false).
- Despawn confirmation (existing): first press sets `_despawnConfirmPending` + 3s timer and relabels the wedge "Confirm?" (hc_radial_despawn_confirm); a second press within 3s despawns.
- Equipped flag: dragging a companion's equipped item to the player goes through InventoryGui -> `Inventory.MoveItemToThis(from, item, amount, x, y)`, which clones ItemData with m_equipped=true (the shift-click path in CompanionInteractPanel.OnCompanionSelected already unequipped first). New `MHR_EquipMove.UnequipIfFromCompanion` + Harmony prefixes `MHR_MoveItemToThis5_Patch` / `MHR_MoveItemToThis2_Patch` (TargetMethod via AccessTools.Method with explicit arg types; try/catch wrapped): if the source inventory is a companion's (and target != source), `Humanoid.UnequipItem(item,false)` then `m_equipped=false` before the clone. check_maxstack.py only recognizes `catch` on its own line after `}`.

### 1.4.88 - fix from the 1.4.87 test (log 9f62d4b3)
- User: smelting OK; cooking made something (log: 5x4 Sausages at the cauldron, 19 put away - one eaten - then 1 more), then Cook off/on and he "freaked out".
- Cause: Cook off -> ToolKeeper unload of the fetched Entrails -> chest on the ledge at y 42 (he's at 40). `HopLine` samples ground from 1.5 m above the last point, so it never saw the 2.8 m ledge: "flat, 0 hops" -> CanReach/PickApproach accepted the ledge spot -> Mover flipped between Direct (straight at the wall) and the hop bridge for a minute. Fix: HopLine also requires the last sample within 1 m of the target's height.
- Reach like a player (user: can open every chest, the ledge ones from the floor): `MHR_Chests.InReach(feet, chest)` = eye (feet + 1.6) within 4 m of chest centre + 0.5 and a clear line (LineClear now tries eye height, then 1.0 m). PickApproach: stand spots from 1.2 m above to 2.6 m below the chest, radii 1.2/1.7/2.2/2.8, accepted when InReach. Used by CanReach, tool/bag trips (arrival) and the farm's AtChestSpot. (Player: m_maxInteractDistance 5 from the eye to the hit point.)
- Cooking notes from the log: catalog 130 recipes / 65 cooking-station items / 20 mead bases; mead focus (bonemass) resolved to MeadBaseHealthMedium, MeadBaseStaminaMedium, MeadBaseFrostResist with no unknown-name warnings.

### 1.4.87 - Cook rewrite (agreed design in the BACKLOG entry "Cook redesign")
- `Jobs/CookController.cs` rewritten. Task picker every 4 s (scan of chests, crafting stations, cooking stations, fermenters within Cooking.Range round the companion): 1) Rescue (a cooking station slot done/burnt) 2) Tap a ready fermenter 3) Store if carrying >= 10 things it made 4) Fill an empty fermenter (focus bases + any base in the bag; finished mead < MeadTarget) 5) Brew a focus base (< MeadBaseTarget, its mead < MeadTarget) 6) Best food (value = food + stamina + eitr): cauldron/prep-table recipes the player knows (`Player.IsRecipeKnown`), food (or what the oven makes of it) below FoodTarget, ingredients ok; or a cooking-station conversion (n = min(free slots, target room, usable raw)); finishing a made item gets +0.5 7) Store what it made.
- Reserve: `Usable(item)` = count in range chests + bag, minus IngredientReserve for raw items (not the output of any cooking recipe/conversion).
- Crafting: walk to a stand spot near the station (PickApproachPoint), require roof (Cover.GetCoverForPoint >= 0.7, like CheckUsable) and fire (private m_haveFire) else the station is skipped for 60 s; PokeInUse, "interact" anim, craft effects, 2.5 s, consume, add item (dropped if bag full).
- Cooking stations: fetch raw (+ up to 3 fuel for an oven), RPC_AddFuel / RPC_AddItem per item, then stand by it: every 0.5 s RPC_RemoveDoneItem for done/burnt slots and pick up drops round the spawn point (only made food, meads, coal); leaves when nothing of its item is still cooking (max 240 s). Slot state read from the ZDO ("slot"+i, "slotstatus"+i: 0 cooking, 1 done, 2 burnt).
- Ledger: what it made/picked up (ZDO HC_CookLedger "prefab:n;"), trimmed to what's still in the bag; storing moves exactly that. Store chest: same item > other made food (Cooking.StoreWithCookedFood) > nearest to the last station used.
- `CookCatalog`: built once from ObjectDB recipes, CookingStation and Fermenter prefabs (mods included); writes BepInEx/config/OfflineCompanionsReborn.Cooking.txt (foods with value + station, mead bases -> meads, stages). MeadFocus stage = last `defeated_<boss>` global key; unknown names in MeadFocus are logged.
- Batches (user): Cooking.BatchSize 5 - crafting repeats up to min(batch, target room / recipe amount, usable ingredients / need) times in one trip (2 s each); cooking stations fill min(free slots, batch, room, usable) slots.
- Settings: [Cooking] Range 25, IngredientReserve 20, FoodTarget 20, BatchSize 5, Foods *, StoreWithCookedFood on, MeadBaseTarget 10, MeadTarget 20, MeadFocus (draft table, user to review).
- Moves through MHR_Mover (tag "Cook") with stand spots; gives up on MHR_Mover.GaveUp or 12 s with no progress.

### 1.4.85 - companion route map (user, 1.4.84 test: "3 doors is not enough", why does the count need a number)
- Valheim's Pathfinding builds a navmesh per agent type from colliders on m_layers; closed doors are colliders, so they're walls, and Humanoid has agentClimb 0.3 m so most stairs/steps don't connect. The door BFS (1.4.84) had to test door combinations with route queries, hence the cap.
- `Patches/MHR_CompanionNavMesh.cs`: postfix on Pathfinding.SetupAgents adds AgentType 40 (copy of Humanoid, agentClimb 0.6); prefix on Pathfinding.BuildTile rebuilds tiles of that type exactly like vanilla (walkable sources + water as area 3, Humanoid swims) but drops every build source whose collider has a Door in its parents. Tiles only get built where GetPath pokes them with that type, i.e. around companions. Any failure -> `Ready = false`, back to Humanoid. Config General.CompanionPathing (default on, restart).
- MHR_Mover (Plan, FindRouteStart, ring search, hop bridge) and MHR_Chests.PickApproach / PickApproachPiece use `MHR_CompanionNavMesh.AgentOrHumanoid`. RouteDoor's own queries stay on Humanoid (it reasons about closed doors) - with the new map it should rarely run. Doors on the route are opened by the existing DoorAhead peek and closed behind as before.
- Steps: `StepEdge` (level 0.3 m ahead, 0.35-1.3 m higher at 0.8 m) hops as soon as he reaches a step, on any route.
- Chest stand spots (user: chests are usable from 2 m): PickApproach rings 1.2/1.7/2.2 m, 16 directions (was 1.2/1.7, 8). Farm counts standing on its picked spot (within 0.7 m, line clear, ReachDist <= UseDistance + 1) as in reach (`AtChestSpot`), for the seed and output chests; tool trips already did (atSpot).
- To check in the log: "[Move] Companion route map ready" at world load; no "couldn't set up" / "failed" warnings.

### 1.4.84 - fixes from the 1.4.83 test (log 991999f4): code done, waiting on the user's build + test
User: not emptying bags; should hop logs and the like instead of going round; stairs hop works; farming better; planting starts in the middle instead of from an edge; got stuck, put sword and shield back on, went back to work, then again and ran off. With Ore on and no ore around he just stood there while the player left (see backlog: job + follow).
- Bags: the log starts in Follow (mode 0) at home carrying TinOre, BoneFragments, RawMeat... from the last session; unload only ran on a job change (0 -> 8 later), and UnloadPending isn't saved. MHR_ToolKeeper now also sets UnloadPending on arriving home (or loading in at home) in mode 0 with something to put away (`State.WasAtHome`).
- Farm store: "[Store] Have crop output but no chest with space found" every scan - the 4 nearest of 24 chests are on the ledge at y 42 (unreachable), the search only tried 4, and ScanNearbyChests cleared the unreachable set every scan. Now up to 12 tries and unreachable chests are remembered for 60 s. User: those chests are in the house, up the stairs, and the door was open - so it's the stairs (not in the navmesh, like the front steps). Also wants any number of doors in a row handled. Now:
  - `MHR_Mover.CanGetTo(pos, goal)`: HopLine straight (< 25 m), or `FindHopBridge` (rings 3/6/10/15/20 m x 12 round the goal: ground point B with HopLine(B, goal) and a full route pos -> B, nearest ring wins, max 24 route queries), or a closed-door chain.
  - PickApproach keeps the stand spots that passed every check but the route and accepts the first (of up to 4) that CanGetTo reaches.
  - Mover.Plan: partial route and no door chain -> detour to the hop bridge; from there Direct mode hops the rest.
  - RouteDoor is now a breadth-first search over door sides, up to 3 closed doors in a row, 8 doors nearest the midpoint, 120 route queries max; returns the first door of the shortest chain (the next plan after walking through finds the next one).
  - HopLine samples ground from 1.5 m above the last ground point (not from above everything), so roofs and upper floors don't count as ground.
- Planting: FindPlantPositionInZone picked the free cell nearest the companion, so he stood still at the zone centre planting in a ring until a spot was 2.2 m off through his own saplings -> "Aborted - stuck moving to plant spot" x3 -> RestoreLoadout (sword + shield back on) each time. Now rows: `GetPlantOrder` per zone (kept until the zone is full) - rows run across the side he comes from, row 0 is the far edge, serpentine along each row; blacklisted spots skipped.
- Logs: Mover direct mode also when the route is > straight + 2 m and HopLine has at least one hop (before: only > 1.6 x straight + 6). StepAhead hop now also on a normal route when held up > 0.6 s (anchor not moved), not swimming.

### 1.4.83 - fixes from the 1.4.82 test (log c0ff32c5): code done, waiting on the user's build + test
User: climbed out of the moat (log: "climbing out at ... steepest step 0.4m" then free); ran around the steps in front of the base instead of jumping them and warped while still in view; shot boars while exploring; shield only equipped in combat; tin mining works; SeneaL_UI blocks the rename box.
- Steps: Follow goal ~4 m away and 2.5 m up the steps, route=full but 85-120 m (navmesh doesn't use the steps). Plan now tries `HopLine` when the route is partial or > 1.6 x straight + 6 m and straight < 25 m: half-meter samples, each rise <= 1.1 m, drop <= 2 m, no water > 0.8 m, chest-height (1.4 m) linecast clear, <= 10 hops. Then the path is just the goal (`S.Direct`), and steering jumps (+4 m/s forward) when `StepAhead` sees a 0.35-1.3 m rise or a knee-high block with clear chest height. Stuck while Direct -> no Direct for 20 s and normal route. Log: "going straight to ... (N step(s) to hop) instead of a Xm way round".
- Warp: it was the plain FollowTeleportDistance (50 m) check in CompanionAI, not FollowWatch. Now skipped while there's line of sight (`MHR_Mover.CanSee`, eye height), unless > 1.5 x the setting.
- Boars: CompanionCombatAI.UpdateTarget re-picks `FindNearbyEnemy` every 2 s while engaged, without the 1.4.79 IsAfterUs rule; after the Neck it moved on to boars. Now filtered too (not in HuntMode, not the current target). The Engage log line now says the distance, the creature's own target and whether it's alerted, to check if boars really were after the player.
- Shield: CompanionSetup.AutoEquipBest had no Shield case, so itemData4 was always null and the shield only came out via the combat weapon logic. Added (best GetBaseBlockPower); the existing code already skips it with a 2H weapon or bow.
- SeneaL_UI rename: not changed. Our name box is a TMP_InputField on a clone of InventoryGui's container; a UI mod that restyles InventoryGui probably covers it or eats the clicks. Fallback is in the BACKLOG (rename fallback + command wheel revisit).

### 1.4.82 - fixes from the 1.4.81 test (log e8ba46da): code done, waiting on the user's build + test
User: chest animation sometimes missing; farm stores after a few crops; planted a few and stopped; Follow ran down to the beach/water again (dry moat = pit with a narrow path); wants the 2.5 m approach hold back; Wood works; fell into the moat and couldn't get out; super recall works when the companion is loaded. Stone and Wood OK.
- Farm storing early: TryNextCropNearby refused while carrying any crop, and UpdateScanning ran TryScanStore first. Now `PackNearlyFull()` (<= 1 empty slot or weight >= Harvest.OverweightThreshold) is the only thing that stops chaining; scan order is store-if-nearly-full, then harvest -> store -> plant (harvest priority) or store -> plant -> harvest (plant priority).
- Planting stopped: "Aborted - stuck moving to plant spot" straight away; a GaveUp from the previous spot survived the soft reset (shift < 4 m, gap < 2 s). The soft reset now clears GaveUp on a new goal (shift > 1.5 m). Ladder/Passes/MainBest are only reset on a new goal too, not on a pause: the harvest swing pauses reset the ladder, so in the moat he sat on "step 1" and never gave up.
- Follow detour: a 67 m route for ~21 m straight. `MHR_Mover.RouteLength` (remaining path length, 0 when not moving); MHR_Follow switches to the trail when it's > 1.8 x straight + 8 m (and > 3 m from the player).
- 2.5 m hold back: `hold = 1.5 s after the player sets off || (player within 2.5 m && not swimming)`. Only blocks starting; never stops a moving companion.
- Moat fall: harvest close-range push at a Beech_Stub on the lip (velY -14.9). `MHR_Mover.DropAhead(pos, dir, dist, maxDrop)`: the push skips it (0.8 m, 1.2 m); Hop sidesteps instead of jumping off a > 1.2 m drop; Sidestep's last-resort perpendicular step checks both sides; `Clear` rejects ground > 1.2 m below.
- Trapped rescue: Plan remembers GoodPos (last spot a full route to the main goal started from, on the ground). On a give-up with the main route partial, >= 2 m below GoodPos, within 40 m and 5 min of it, not swimming or in combat: `CompanionAI.MHR_PlaceAt(GoodPos)` and the move restarts. Log: "trapped at ... moving back to ...".
- Climb out (user: "behave like a player"): Character.GetSlideAngle is 90 for non-players (they never slide) and OnCollisionStay counts normal.y > 0.1 as ground, so jump-climbing steep faces works for NPCs. On the first stuck check with the main route partial and the target (GoodPos, else the goal) > 1.5 m above: `FindClimb` samples ground heights along 16 directions, 1 m steps to 16 m, stops at deep water, a dip > 1.5 m, a non-terrain rise > 1 m (building wall/roof) or a rise > 6 m; picks the smallest steepest-step (ties: shorter), skipping spots already tried. `Climb` faces it, sprints, jumps on the ground when the face ahead rises or forward speed < 1, adds 5 m/s forward on each jump; rests (halts) below 15% stamina until 60% or 5 s; gives up a way after 5 s with no 0.3 m height gain, up to 3 ways per goal; then the normal ladder -> give-up -> trapped rescue. Harvest push is off while climbing (`MHR_Mover.Climbing`). Logs: "climbing out at", "climbed out", "couldn't climb out at".
- Chest animation: Farm and Smelt chest opens and MHR_ToolKeeper chest trips trigger "interact" (Cook/Homestead already did).

### 1.4.80 - Stone: one hit per rock (user report; the uploaded log was the 1.4.78 one again)
- Destructible.Damage instantiates m_spawnWhenDamaged (the cracked `_frac` rock) at the same transform and the original object goes away, so `_target` became null after the first hit -> "Target DESTROYED after 1 hits" -> drop collection found nothing (nothing had dropped) -> next rock. The user saw a damaged rock left behind each time.
- `HarvestController.TryFollowCrackedRock`: when a MineRock/MineRock5/Destructible target vanishes, look for a rock object (not a Piece/Character) whose root is within 0.5 m of the old target position (`_lastTargetPos`, updated every attacking frame) and carry on with it: reclassify, re-claim, reset swing/attempt counters and the locked move/face targets. Log: "Rock cracked - carrying on with ...". If none: the old destroyed -> drops path.

### 1.4.78 - fixes from the 1.4.77 test (log 32578da5): code done, waiting on the user's build + test
User report: farmer "went crazy"; Wander-off follow stutters close up; Wood job stood around (combat); chased something in the moat; Stone swings didn't hit; repair trip when not home with gear > 90%, through water. Catching + Wander toggle OK. Seat warnings gone (0).
- Farm chaos = store trip to a chest 10 m away at (-265.6, 42.0, -311.8): GetPath to the chest centre is partial (inside the chest) -> RouteDoor picked the wood gate 30 m away -> through it, still partial -> same gate again from the other side, x6. Fixes: FarmController.ChestGoal uses MHR_Chests.PickApproach (walks onto the spot, dist 0.5); MHR_Mover skips the door it routed through in the last 30 s (S.LastRouteDoor).
- Farm sidestepping between crops: Mover state persisted across 2 m crop hops (fresh only on > 4 m shift / > 2 s gap), so time spent harvesting counted as "standing still". Now: gap > 0.5 s, or goal shift > 1.5 m (not Follow), resets the stuck clocks and ladder.
- Combat: RtD crab/dolphin are MonsterAI, so MHR_Threat.IsPassive missed them. Now passive while not targeting/alerted if BaseAI.m_passiveAggresive or MonsterAI m_avoidLand / m_fleeIfNotAlerted. CompanionCombatAI drops a target > 40 m away (CombatLeash, not in HuntMode) and a passive one that isn't targeting anyone. The dolphin at 141 m had kept him engaged, so HarvestController (returns while IsInCombat) never started Wood.
- Stone: 20 swings from 1.9 m, none connected. 1.4.77 removed HarvestController's close-range push (MoveTowards target when < 4 m and vel < 1); the reapproach after a miss relies on it. Put back.
- Jobs vs Follow (user): MHR_JobRules.OnJobSet - a job turns Follow off (ZDO HC_FollowPausedByJob), mode 0 turns it back on if the job did it; radial ToggleFollow on with a job -> SetActionMode(0). Applied once at load for saves with both on (EnsureActionModeMigration).
- Repair: MHR_Nav.RepairThreshold "any wear" only within min(25 m, HomeTripRange) of home (was NearHome = 60 m). RepairController walks to MHR_Chests.PickApproachPiece (spot by the station a route ends at, radii 1.3/1.8/2.2) instead of the station centre (partial route -> swam across instead of the door).
- Follow stutter (Wander off, close): lead = spot + travel x max(1.5 m, speed x 1 s), arrival 0.3 m, ease off a pace when ahead of the spot.

### 1.4.77 - Follow tweaks + phase 3 part 1 (Farm, gathering): code done, waiting on the user's build + test
- User (after 1.4.76): hard to catch a following companion to open its inventory/wheel. Now it holds for 1 s after the player sets off (T.MovingSince) and while the player is within 2.5 m.
- User: tie Follow's moving around to the Wander toggle (radial wheel, per companion, ZDO HC_Wander, default off). User chose: switch it on once for all companions (`HC_WanderFollowMigrated`, in `CompanionSetup.EnsureActionModeMigration`; new companions get it too). Wander on = spots re-picked every 10-25 s + idle shuffling; off = keeps its spot (re-picked only if it becomes invalid) and stands still. Regroup and indoor sitting apply either way. Side effect accepted: Homestead companions with Wander on roam around home (existing Homestead Wander behaviour).
- Phase 3 part 1: `MHR_Nav.Move` (MoveFarm/MovePlant) now calls `MHR_Mover.Go` (Plant keeps its stand-on-the-field rule; the farm checks reach itself before moving). Old jump tracker in MHR_Nav removed. `HarvestController` (Wood/Stone/Ore/Forage) moves to targets and drops through `MHR_Mover.Go(..., "Gather")`; the nav-retry wait and close-range push are gone (they'd fight the Mover). Its 10 s give-up timer counts at 0.3x while `MHR_Mover.Progressing` (routing around something) and gives up at once when `MHR_Mover.GaveUp` (after 0.5 s on a target). Kept: the push when "arrived" on the ground but the target is still out of reach above; the in-attack MoveToPoint re-approach for low targets (short range).
- Still on old movement: MHR_Nav.MoveWalk users (Homestead, Cook, Smelt, Repair-buildings/Restock in CompanionAI) = part 2; Hunt, Fish = part 3. Farm's own 10 s stuck/blacklist and DoorHandler stay until phase 4.

### 1.4.76 - tested by the user (log 0f7e80ce): "everything working as expected"
- Loads clean (58 methods patched). Follow seen in the log: opened wood gates/doors on the way ("no way through - heading for the closed $piece_woodgate" -> opened), one sidestep unstick, sat on $piece_throne01, got up when the player moved, regrouped after 56 s.
- Fixed after the test: 2,779 Unity warnings "Setting linear velocity of a kinematic body is not supported" while seated. Character.UpdateWalking sets m_body.linearVelocity every physics tick even when attached, so a kinematic body warns each tick. The seat is now held without kinematic: gravity off, collisions with the chair piece ignored (like ship seats), position pinned every frame; restored on getting up.
- Probably the same warning flood with ship seats (`CompanionAI.AttachToShip` sets the body kinematic) - pre-existing, not checked; fix the same way when ships come up.

### 1.4.76 - phase 2, Follow on MHR_Mover
Design approved by the user (with: regroup is a toggle + timer; indoors stay closer and sit, emptying bags first near home).
- New `AI/MHR_Follow.cs`. CompanionAI's follow branch calls `MHR_Follow.Update` for Player targets; non-player targets still use `FollowWithFormation`.
- Per followed player (`T`): travel direction from position change (smoothed; not camera facing), speed, still-since, a trail of the last 40 positions (0.75 m apart, cleared on teleport), indoors = local player `InShelter()` (checked every 0.5 s).
- Per companion (`S`): a spot as angle/radius around the travel direction. 15% ahead (20-60 deg), 30% beside (60-100), 55% behind-side (100-170); radius 3 m to 0.7 x MaxDistance outdoors, 1.5 m+ indoors. Re-picked every 10-25 s, re-checked every 1 s. Spot needs ground (normal.y >= 0.6, within 2.5 m of player height, water depth <= 1 m), line of sight from the player's head, and spacing from other companions' spots (3 m, 1.5 m indoors). No valid spot -> trail mode: a point 2 m + 1.5 m x rank back along the player's path; retried every 1-3 s.
- Moving: starts when > 4 m from the spot (2.5 m indoors), > MaxDistance from the player, or swimming. Goal leads the spot by 0.5 s of player travel so it doesn't stop-start. Speed (new `MHR_Mover.Go(..., speed)`): match the player (walk/sneak, jog, run), one step faster when > 3 m off the spot, run when > 8 m off or > MaxDistance + 4. Sneak animation follows the player (`CompanionAI.MHR_SetCrouch`). Mover gave up -> vanilla `Follow` (`MHR_FollowFallback`) until it retries.
- Player still (> 1 s): within range -> stop where it is; idle every 6-14 s (35% turn to look at the player, else walk to a point within 2 m / 1 m indoors of where it stopped). Too far or swimming -> go to a spot within 0.6 x range. Regroup (`Follow/Regroup`, `RegroupTime` 45 s): one move to a spot 2-4 m (1.5-2.5 m indoors) around the player.
- Indoors + still for 5-10 s (random per companion) + `Follow/SitIndoors`: first `MHR_ToolKeeper.RequestUnload` (only with a home set and near it; same rules as after a job change), wait 2.5 s and while a trip or repair runs; then the nearest free `Chair` (not m_inShip, within range+1.5 m of the player, same level, in sight of the player, not in use by a player or claimed by another companion) -> walk there, attach (pinned to m_attachPoint, kinematic, `m_attachAnimation` bool) - weapons stay out like vanilla chairs (HideHandItems would make auto-equip fight it). No chair (or 15 s / gave up -> 20 s cool-down) -> `emote_sit` on the floor facing the player. Companions without player animations don't sit.
- `MHR_Follow.Tick` (from MHR_ToolKeeper.TickOne, owner only) gets them up: player moved > 2.5 m from where they sat down (1 m outdoors), follow target changed, combat, job/Homestead set, ToolKeeper trip, repair, CompanionRest resting/sleep (floor sit keeps the emote for Rest), or the follow branch not running for 1 s. Chair: steps off the front onto the floor (vanilla detach offset if there's no floor).
- `MHR_Mover`: `Go` overload with speed (Auto/Walk/Jog/Run); re-plan within 0.3 s when the goal drifts > 1.5 m from the planned destination (moving goals); tag "Follow" ignores the "no closer for 8 s" stuck rule (keeping pace isn't stuck), still-for-2.5 s still applies.
- `DoorHandler` stays idle while `MHR_Follow.Owns` (follow ran in the last 0.5 s). Old follow water branch no longer used for players.
- Config (new section Follow): MaxDistance 10, IndoorDistance 4, Regroup on, RegroupTime 45, SitIndoors on. AI/FormationOffset and AI/FormationCatchupDist unused for players - remove in the config cleanup.
- Things to watch in testing: stop-start while walking; spots in dense forest/bases (LOS rejects -> trail mode); chairs on raised floors or benches; sitting animation for thrones/benches from mods (unknown animator bool = a Unity warning).

### Release plan (user, 2026-10-04)
- No packaged release until the movement update (phases 2-4) is done. Each step is merged to `main` and tested as a version bump with the user's own build; package and release everything at the end.

### Phase 2 - Follow on MHR_Mover: the user's answers (2026-10-04)
1. Companions move around while following, not locked to one slot. Goal: behave like a real player.
2. Up to ~10 m away (including ahead of the player) is fine.
3. Several companions spread out around the player instead of a line behind.
4. Player stops: companions stay where they are; they may wander a little, and come back to the player if the player stays still for an extended period.
5. Only combat should behave differently. Combat itself (not always running off to engage, player-like strategies) is a later polish pass - not part of phase 2.

- Seen, not new: the old `DoorHandler` logs "stuck=11.0s" (Debug) while the farmer stands still harvesting - it counts working in place as stuck. Harmless; goes away when phase 3 retires the per-job stuck code.

### 1.4.75 - code (branch `claude/relaxed-archimedes-ecoh6h`)
What was built (all items below):
- (1) `CompanionAI` (end of the follow/idle branch): job mode on (`MHR_JobRules.IsJobMode`: not Follow 0, not legacy Stay 4) and no job driving -> `StopMoving()` instead of `EnforceHomePatrol`/`IdleMovement`.
- (2) `FarmController.TryNextCropNearby()` after drops are collected: if nothing needs storing (`HasCropOutput()` false), take the nearest ripe crop within `NextCropRange` (Farm, default 3 m, 0 = off) straight to MovingToPickable. Otherwise the old 2 s idle + full scan (so store trips are unchanged).
- (3a) `MHR_Mover.Plan`: GetPath fails outright -> `FindRouteStart` tries 8 directions at 1.5 m and 2.5 m (clear at knee height, footing, not deep water) for a spot a route starts from; steps there as a 3 s detour. Throttled to every 2 s. (3b) `MHR_Mover.Tick`: a door we opened isn't closed while the mover is active and stuck (`Ladder > 0` or a detour) within 8 m of it.
- (4) `FindRipePickable` logs one summary line per scan (counts of picked/claimed, not crops, unreachable) instead of per crop. The per-crop `cultivated=` detail is gone; add it back temporarily if the auto-farm-zone investigation needs it.
- (5) `[BepInIncompatibility]` removed. `CompanionsPlugin.Awake` checks `Chainloader.PluginInfos` for `com.profmags.companions` (loads first: GUIDs load alphabetically) and logs an error and doesn't start. `Start()` re-checks after all plugins loaded and shuts down (unpatch, destroy ConfigPanel/FarmZonePlacer) if needed.
- (6) CHANGELOG 1.4.53-1.4.74 rewritten one line per item; 1.4.75 entry added. (7) README: Note 2, Recent Highlights v1.4.75 (show the user before packaging), install note about removing the original, Source section.
- Also in 1.4.75: the Harmony attribute fix from the C# conversion. `CombatPatches.EquipBestWeapon_PreferHighestDamage.Postfix` only acts outside Balanced/Ranged/Melee (`WeaponLogic.IsManagedStance`), i.e. Passive: swaps to the highest effective-damage weapon. In 1.4.74 it never ran and `Prefix` ran a second time as a postfix.

### 1.4.75 - original proposal (from the 1.4.74 farm/repair log)
1. **Idle wander during jobs:** after each crop the farm job paused 2 s with nothing driving him, so the vanilla idle wander walked him back to his idle point (~4 m behind the farm edge, around (-275,-326)), then he walked back in for the next crop: walk in, pick, walk back, every crop. Follow was off. Fix: while a job is on, a short pause means standing still, not idle wander. No job on = wander as before.
2. **Harvest like a player working a row:** after picking a crop, go straight to the next ripe one within a few meters, no pause and no full rescan. The full scan runs only when nothing ripe is close.
3. **Doorways (repair trip, ~45 s lost):** he opened the door, stood in the doorway and couldn't get a route from there; the door got closed behind him and reopened while he was still trying. Fix: (a) if no route can start from where he stands (e.g. a doorway), step 1-2 m to find one right away instead of running the whole unstuck ladder; (b) don't close a door he opened while he's still stuck near it.
4. **Farm scan log:** one summary line per scan instead of one line per crop (60-80 crops, ~460 Debug lines per scan).
5. **Original-mod check:** remove `[BepInIncompatibility("com.profmags.companions")]` (Hexium lists it as an incompatibility tag). Replace with a startup check: if ProfMags' original is loaded, log a clear error saying to remove it (it doesn't work on Valheim 1.0) and don't start Reborn. Same note in the README and the Nexus description.
6. **Changelog:** rewrite 1.4.53-1.4.74 in the one-line style (see the CHANGELOG style rule below).
7. **README:** add the user's "Note 2" next to the existing note (text below).
- Not in 1.4.75: moving Follow onto MHR_Mover is phase 2. Order after that: Follow (ask the user for their follow ideas first) -> jobs move over -> remove the old per-job stuck code.

### Packaging (agreed with the user)
- Only `Companions.dll` is needed to run. Thunderstore needs `manifest.json`, `README.md`, `icon.png` (256x256) in the zip root; `CHANGELOG.md` is optional (shows as a Changelog tab). Hexium: packaged the same way as Thunderstore. Nexus: DLL only, the description lives on the page.
- `LICENSE` goes in every package, Nexus included (MIT: ProfMags' copyright notice must ship with copies).
- The source now lives in this GitHub repo. The user doesn't mind including source in packages but most players won't care. The repo is **private**, so don't point the README or Nexus at it unless the user makes it public.

## BACKLOG (come back to - check with the user before building any of it)
- **Fish job: craft bait (user, 2026-10-07):** when the Fish job moves to MHR_Mover (phase 3 part 3), let the companion craft fishing bait like a player - keep a stock of each bait it needs (configurable target, same style as the Cooking settings: reserve for raw ingredients, batch size). Today it only fetches bait from chests near home (MHR_ToolKeeper K_BAIT); the cooking job never makes bait.
- **When a job runs out of work (user, 2026-10-07):** don't just stand around - act like a player would. Ideas to discuss: go back to following, tidy/sort chests, top up fires, repair gear, help another job, wander near home. (Related: jobs + Follow together.)
- **User's to-do notes:** the user has more notes to add (some already covered/fixed) - merge them here when they arrive. Guiding rule: make the companion behave like a player.
- **Cook and Homestead review (user, 1.4.86):** both need a feature review before moving them onto MHR_Mover (phase 3 part 2 continued). Leave them alone unless something is really off.
- **Cook redesign (agreed design, 2026-10-07 - build after Smelt is tested; expect several rounds of refinement).**
  Current behaviour reviewed with the user: cauldron only, random recipe, no limits, fake crafting (ingredients removed, item added after 3 s, no fire/roof check), stores any non-equipped food to the nearest chest with a free slot, 25 m round wherever he is, fermenter claim (two companions don't head for the same empty one).
  Decisions:
  1. **Which food:** among recipes the player knows (`Player.IsRecipeKnown` - vanilla learns a recipe once all its ingredients were picked up), whose output is below its stock target AND every ingredient is above its reserve: make the best one (highest health + stamina, eitr too).
  - **Almost every number is a config setting** (user): reserves, stock targets, range, mead amounts, store rules, food list, boss -> mead table.
  2. **Raw ingredient reserve 20** (config): counted over all nearby chests (+ his bag); he may only take from an ingredient while there are more than 20, so the player always has enough left to make something specific. Cooked-food stock target: config too (default to agree - 20?).
  3. **All the cooking stations, like a player** (user: the best food usually comes from the oven; small outposts often only have a cooking station): cauldron, food preparation table (both CraftingStations), cooking station, iron cooking station and oven (CookingStations: RPC_AddItem, watch the cook timer, RPC_RemoveDoneItem before it burns, oven fuel via RPC_AddFuel). Chains: prep table makes the uncooked item (dough, raw pie...) that the oven turns into the food - plan the whole chain for the target food. No "cauldron first" ban (dropped): the limits do the job - he only makes a food while it's below its stock target and every ingredient is above its reserve, so grilling meat stops at the cooked-meat target and never eats into the last 20 raw. Best food first, so stew (if carrots exist) wins over grilled meat.
  4. **Cauldron like a player:** stations aren't locked in Valheim (only chests are) - walk into use range, `PokeInUse` (in-use effect), crafting animation, require fire + roof like `CraftingStation.CheckUsable`.
  5. **Storing:** like bag-emptying - chest that already has that food, else one with other cooked food, else the chest nearest the cooking area. Configurable.
  6. **His own food:** keep a per-item count of what he cooked and hasn't stored (ZDO); store exactly that. The rest is his to eat (stacks merge, so tags on items aren't reliable).
  7. **Range:** 25 m round where he is (config).
  8. **Food list (config):** default everything cookable (vanilla + mods); the player can limit it. Idea: same style as the gather lists - `*` = all, names to allow, `-Name` to exclude - and write a generated reference file at startup listing every cookable food (prefab + display name, mods included) so players can copy names; the config description points to it.
  - **Mead:** prepare for the NEXT boss. Keep 10 of each relevant mead base (config) and 20 of each finished mead (config). Which meads = a boss -> meads table in the config (user examples: Eikthyr dead -> poison resistance + minor healing; Bonemass dead -> frost resistance + medium healing). Draft defaults for every boss from the game's item names and show the user before building.
- **Jobs + Follow together (user, 1.4.84 test):** turning Follow off for a job (1.4.78) is fine for home jobs (farm, smelt, cook...) but not for Wood/Stone/Ore out in the field: with no ore around he stood still while the player left, and Follow had to be turned back on. Wanted: a gathering job can run with Follow on - work what's near, and when there's nothing to do, go back to following the player (and pick up the job again near the next targets). Probably per job: field jobs (Wood, Stone, Ore, Forage, Hunt?) keep Follow; home jobs turn it off. Talk through with the user before building.
- **Command wheel revisit (user, 1.4.83):** the wheel needs a rethink before more goes on it. Candidates: Rename, a guard / patrol mode, and late-game features from the base game. The user isn't sure how much more belongs on the wheel; consider a sub-menu or moving some items elsewhere. Talk it through with the user before building.
- **Rename fallback (user agreed it's probably the fix, 1.4.83):** SeneaL_UI (hexium.gg/mods/seneaL/SeneaL_UI) blocks the panel's name box. Rename through the vanilla `TextInput` dialog (like signs: implement `TextReceiver`, `TextInput.instance.RequestText(receiver, topic, 24)`), opened from outside the inventory panel. Where it lives depends on the command wheel revisit.
- **Other gather drops by limit (user, 1.4.64):** survey what else gathering targets drop (seeds from foraged/gathered plants, feathers, etc.) and pick them up according to their own gather limits instead of the current "not on the list = leave it".
- **Piles (Part B, agreed design in 1.4.64 notes):** UsePiles toggle, shared pile zones, placement priority, hammer via ToolKeeper, chest fallback. Confirm stack capacities/pieces in-game first.
- **Forage / food limits:** same list + category-default treatment as GatherWood (ForageLimit, FoodLimit).
- **Auto farm zones:** mark hoed ground near home as farmland. First find out why MHR_SafeCultivated says "not cultivated" for planted crops.
- **Chest approach point for other jobs:** Farm (stuck going to the output chest), Smelt, Cook, Restock and Homestead still aim at the chest centre - reuse MHR_Chests.PickApproach/LineClear (1.4.66, tool trips only so far). Same for MHR_Nav jump assist in other jobs.
- **Auto-add modded items (user, 1.4.67 - do later, "certainly worth doing for those running mods"):** toggle `AutoAddModItems` (General, default on). At startup scan the game and add NON-VANILLA items (hard-code vanilla names per category so removing a vanilla item from a list keeps it removed) to: PlantAny (cultivator plantables with m_needCultivatedGround - trees/saplings don't need it), GatherWood (TreeBase/TreeLog drops), GatherStone/GatherOre (MineRock/MineRock5/Destructible drops; ore = source needs a higher pickaxe tier), forage/food (Pickable drops). Category default limit; log once what was added; user's config line not rewritten. User has a Jotunn farming mod adding Seed_*_RtD (Cabbage, Chantenay, Corn, Cucumber, Garlic, Onion, Potato, Pumpkin, Radish, RedBeet, Rice, Tomato, Watermelon, Wheat).
- **Pathing / movement overhaul (user, after 1.4.70 - once every job is confirmed working):** one movement layer for all jobs, follow, bed and doors (today: MHR_Nav for Farm/Tools only, MoveWalk elsewhere, DoorHandler and CompanionRest separate). Include: obstacle hop (raycast ahead at knee height while moving, jump toward it while running), repeated hops up steep inclines like a player, stairs, reach-range rules everywhere (MHR_Nav.ReachDist), chest approach spots for every job (MHR_Chests.PickApproach), door handling that works from stairs. 1.4.70 log 9a7c4a34: with the house door closed, bed navigation failed 3x - DoorHandler "Approach timeout (10s)" at 3.3-3.5m from the door at the top of the stairs (door y 40.9, companion y ~38.3); door opened fine from inside in the morning. Unload worked (Dandelion, Iron put away in a chest already holding them); wake position correct.
- **Overhaul ideas from MidgardPlus review (user, 1.4.73):** unstuck ladder = sidestep back-diagonal (knee-height raycast) -> jump toward the next path corner -> re-plan -> give up and skip the target for a few minutes; proactive door open on stall + keep close-behind; follow hysteresis (start ~3.8 m / stop ~2.6 m) with walk/jog/run bands; shared job runner (choose target / act, common stuck watchdog + skip map); "need" reasons in hover text; speech rate limits with a queue for important lines. Optional safe catch-up teleport beside the player for Follow only (undecided).
- **Water (user, 1.4.73 log 5cd19a7a):** he already skips crossings / won't chase into swim-depth water when stamina is low. Wants: jump narrow streams to stay dry (no Wet), run-jump into deep water to get a head start swimming. Goes into the movement overhaul.
- **Crossbow never fires (1.4.73 log 5cd19a7a):** vanilla `Humanoid.IsWeaponLoaded()` always returns false (only Player overrides it, with its reload action), and `Attack.Start` refuses a m_requiresReload weapon that isn't loaded. Companions equip the arbalest (TryForceRangedWeapon "ready, will equip") but can never shoot; bolts equipped by hand don't help. Fix: per-companion loaded flag via Harmony on IsWeaponLoaded/ResetLoadedWeapon + a reload step (m_reloadAnimation, m_reloadTime, reload stamina) when a crossbow is out and unloaded; FindReadyBow must match ammo to m_ammoType (bolts vs arrows).
- **Follow like a player (user, 1.4.73):** don't trail directly behind all the time - drift to one side, sometimes ahead. User has more thoughts on follow; ask before designing it.
- **Job refuses when it can't work (user, after 1.4.70):** when a job can't run (broken/missing tool and too far from home), turn the job off and fall back to Follow; if the player turns it back on while that's still true, refuse with a reason ("I need an axe and we're too far from home", "My axe broke").
- **Speech tied to actions (user, 1.4.70):** lines for what the companion is doing (emptying bags, fetching tools, heading to bed, planting...), several variations per action so it stays fresh. Only `hc_speech_unload` ("I need to empty my bags first.") exists so far.
- **Config cleanup (user, 1.4.65):** regroup sections (General, Controls, Homestead, Harvest, Farm, Tools...) and make descriptions consistent. One pass right before a release; renaming sections resets users' changed values, so note it in the changelog.
- **Self defense everywhere (user, 1.4.65):** "self defense overrules almost everything but passive mode" - make every job and Homestead chore drop into combat when attacked and resume after, consistently (tool trips already do as of 1.4.65).
- **Guide:** rename "Stay Home" to "Homestead" in the Command Wheel Guide; describe job/Homestead exclusivity, tool-trip rules, PlantAny/SeedMinimum/SeedTarget/PlantGroupSize.
- **Problems from the Command Wheel Guide (user is reviewing and will send thoughts):** Pickup toggle does nothing; recall leaves Stay Home on; Hunt targets tamed animals; Forage filter likely skips wild barley/flax; any build tool counts as a hammer for Repair/Homestead; Restock/Smelt/Cook/Homestead chest searches don't skip private chests/carts/ships; Fish ignores biome and never stores fish; dead settings (General > MaxLeashDistance, Homestead ScanInterval/TaskSlotTime/SmeltSlotTime/ScanBackoff, ~27 combat entries).
- **Before release:** check every job's Info-level logging is quiet for players (Farm done in 1.4.63); bump manifests; show the user the README "Recent Highlights" (5 bullets) before packaging; README "What This Update Changes" + Nexus BBCode description.
- Older items: compact bug-report file (OfflineCompanions_Report.txt); Haldor recruit without Trader Overhaul; per-companion height; OverheadBarsMode; stance feel; F1/F8 settings "sometimes not applying".

Current version: **1.4.74** — build tag `v92-movement-core`. Verified: branches 0/1921, maxstack 0/1921 (dead code: old IsPassiveAnimal bodies + the 1.4.73 leftovers), check_refs 0, ResolveRefs 18630/18630, JIT 1856/1856, version 1.4.74. Untested in-game.
- **1.4.74 = movement overhaul phase 1** (user confirmed the plan after the 1.4.73 logs 25f30bda / 5cd19a7a). New file MHR_Mover.cs (classes MHR_Mover, MHR_Water, MHR_Threat, MHR_Crossbow, MHR_WeaponLoaded_Patch, MHR_ResetLoaded_Patch), compiled with its own stubs and spliced after MHR_Equip. Patch script: scratchpad/patch74.py (run on Companions_source_1473_pre74.il).
  - MHR_Mover.Go(ai, dt, goal, dist, allowRun, tag): Pathfinding.GetPath(Humanoid, requireFull=false) re-planned every 1s, corner following via BaseAI.MoveTowards, arrival = flat <= dist and |dy| <= 1.5. Walk < 2.5m left, jog, run beyond WorkRunDistance. Partial route -> RouteDoor (closed valid door within 35m of the goal with a full path to one side, far side closer to the goal) -> open from DoorDist <= 2.2 -> 5s "detour" leg through -> re-plan. DoorAhead raycast every 0.4s opens a closed door in reach on the way. Opened doors close behind (>3.5m, no player within 2.5m, nobody in the doorway) in MHR_Mover.Tick.
  - Stuck = still 2.5s or no progress along the leg 8s. Ladder: door near (2.6m) / sidestep back-diagonal (knee raycast + footing + not deep water) -> hop toward next corner -> sidestep other side -> hop -> detour (side point with full path) -> hop; 2 passes without getting 1.5m closer to the real goal -> GaveUp (retry allowed after 15s). Progress on the main leg resets the ladder.
  - Water: DepthAt = Floating.GetWaterLevel - ground raycast; swims when depth > m_swimDepth-0.4 (companions m_swimDepth 2 -> 1.6m; vanilla Wet only at swim depth). Plan(): swim meters on route; if stamina < need (capped 90% max) -> HumanoidAvoidWater full path, else TooTired -> WaitStamina at the edge (max 40s). Streams <= 3m wide jumped (Leap 6.5); wide water: running dive in (not when tired). Swimming with no/partial route -> FindShore (bank nearest the goal, not a wall > 1m above the water), every 0.5s.
  - Wired: ToolKeeper Drive (tags Tools/Repair), trip stall = GaveUp or (45s no straight-line progress and mover not progressing), TripTimeout 150; StartTrip/StartStationTrip/EndTrip Release; station goal frozen at trip start; atSpot = flat 1.0 + dy 1.5. CompanionAI.UpdateAI bed walk (IL_02ae Go "Bed", arrival 1.2); CompanionRest.UpdateNavigation keeps _navTimeout >= 6 while MHR_Mover.KeepAlive. RepairController.UpdateMoving Go "Repair" + stuck block -> StuckSeconds (11 when GaveUp). UpdateAI IL_0121: UpdateFurnitureUnstuck/GroundStuckRecovery/ProactiveJump skipped while OwnsMovement. DoorHandler.Update returns early when idle and the mover owns movement.
  - Low stamina: UpdateAI IL_00ea -> MHR_Water.ApplyAgent (HumanoidAvoidWater only when the straight line to the follow target crosses swim-depth water; was: any water below 50% stamina = the "avoids water it could walk through" report).
  - Combat: IsPassiveAnimal (CompanionAI + CompanionCombatAI) -> MHR_Threat.IsPassive (Deer/Hare/Chicken, any AnimalAI, temporarily ignored targets). WaterSafety.ShouldAvoidChase -> MHR_Threat.AvoidChase: only when he'd have to swim at the target's spot; keeps fighting if the target is alerted/targeting and he has a ready ranged weapon, or it's within 3m; otherwise ignore 20s + Disengage (ignore drops once it targets someone or leaves deep water).
  - Repairs: RepairController.UpdateIdle station distance -> MHR_Nav.StationScanDist (near home: any station within HomeTripRange of home, distance x0.01). ToolKeeper: failed repair trip -> RepairFailUntil[kind] 300s -> chest -> none: SpeakBroke + follow.
  - Crossbow: Humanoid.IsWeaponLoaded is ldc.i4.0 for non-players; Attack.Start refuses m_requiresReload weapons that aren't loaded. Postfix returns MHR_Crossbow.IsLoaded; ResetLoadedWeapon postfix unloads; MHR_Crossbow.Tick (from ToolKeeper.TickOne) plays m_reloadAnimation bool for Lerp(reloadTime, reloadTime/2, skill), uses m_reloadStaminaDrain, then SetTrigger(anim+"_done"). WeaponLogic.FindReadyBow/BalancedPrefix Ready -> MHR_Crossbow.ReadyFor (needs matching m_ammoType ammo).
  - Config: General.FollowStuckTeleport (bool, false) -> MHR_Mover.FollowWatch before FollowWithFormation (15s without moving 1.5m, > 10m away, no line of sight, not fighting -> TeleportToFollowTarget, made assembly).
  - Reviewed by a separate agent; fixed: ladder restart loop, endless bank wait, stopping short of the goal at the path end, tired dive, per-frame shore search, stale GaveUp at trip start, trip stall fallback, ignore list on attackers.
  - Phase 2 (next): Follow on the mover (hysteresis 3.8/2.6, walk/jog/run bands, side/front positions - user has more thoughts, ask first), then phase 3 jobs, phase 4 delete old per-job stuck code (MoveToPoint ContextSteer fallback, DoorHandler proactive, UpdateGroundStuckRecovery etc.).

Job never switched off.

Pile spot = 6m from the ore (_attackNodePos) rotated 50 degrees off the companion's side, ground height, reused within 25m; TickDodge drives the walk (MoveToPoint walk, 1.5m, 25s timeout), DropStone drops every Stone stack with m_autoPickup=false.

TryFindNearbyLog -> `MHR_GatherRules.NearestLog` (nearest TreeLog within 12m, not blacklisted, AllowTarget). Junk is still dropped from the scan (not moved to arrival) - left as is.

- FaceTarget's Transform.set_rotation -> `MHR_Nav.TurnTo` (RotateTowards 540 deg/s).

Previous version: **1.4.70** — build tag `v88-unload-sprint-reach`. Verified: branches 0/1822, maxstack 0/1822, check_refs 0, ResolveRefs 17869/17869, JIT 1761/1761, version 1.4.70. Untested in-game.

Seed/output chests at y 42.0 with companion at 40.0 = stacked chests; the 1.4.68 level rule (stop distance 0.5 when |dy|>1) made him try to stand at chest height -> jumping/spinning in a corner, blacklists. User: running drains all stamina; on job change unload to chests near home keeping only equipment/food/mead the PLAYER gave (tools/armor/food they own kept; wood etc. the player gave still stored); Farm -> Follow should unload first; speech per action later.
- Reach: new `MHR_Nav.ReachDist(a,b)` = flat distance if |dy| <= 2.2 else 3D; replaces Vector3.Distance in FarmController.UpdateMovingToSeedChest/OutputChest/Pickable. Level rule now only for tag "Plant" (`MHR_Nav.MovePlant`, used by UpdateMovingToPlantSpot, which keeps its 3D check).
- Seed sources: `MHR_SeedRules.NeededForSeeds(farm, veg)` (seed source whose seed stock < SeedTarget) -> WantsStore/StoreOutput treat it like a plantable (keep up to max(SeedMinimum, SeedCarry)).
- Sprint bursts: Harmony prefix `MHR_SprintBurst_Patch` on Character.SetRun(bool) -> `MHR_Sprint.Filter`: companions only (cached GetComponent<CompanionSetup>); pct = CompanionStamina.Stamina/MaxStamina; running -> resting at <= 50% (<= 30% when CompanionAI.IsInCombat); resting -> run allowed again at >= 90% (or in combat while > 30%). Covers every SetRun path (follow, MoveToPoint, combat MoveTo). (Spliced IL: monodis prints [0Harmony]; replaced with ['0Harmony'] to match the main extern.)
- Unload: in MHR_ToolKeeper. Ownership tag ItemData.m_customData["HC_Own"]="1" on "keepable" items (weapon/bow/shield/armor/utility/tool/torch/ammo/consumable/trinket types) - applied to everything in the pack at first tick after load and every tick while CompanionInteractPanel.IsOpenFor(cs) (the player gives items through that panel). Unload candidates = not equipped, not (keepable && HC_Own), not a ToolKeeper-borrowed prefab, and when the new mode is Farm (8) not a plantable. Trigger: ZDO ActionMode differs from last tick, or StayHome turned on -> UnloadPending. Decision loop after borrowed returns: if pending and within 40m of home (or no home) -> `ChestFor` (reachable usable chest within 30m of home already holding that prefab with room, else nearest reachable with room) -> trip phase 3 (speech hc_speech_unload, 120s throttle); at the chest `Deposit` moves the target item plus every other candidate that already has a stack there. Repeats (max 10 trips) until nothing left; IsBusy also true while UnloadActive (jobs/follow wait, bed waits). Far from home -> stays pending until back within 40m.

Previous version: **1.4.67** — build tag `v85-seed-lookup-chest-reach`. Verified: branches 0/1802, maxstack 0/1802, check_refs 0, ResolveRefs 17770/17770, JIT 1742/1742, version 1.4.67. Untested in-game.
- User log fa467f2d (1.4.66): PickSeed chose CarrotSeeds (so MHR_Chests counting works), but every zone said `no "CarrotSeeds" in chests either`. Root cause (ProfMags original): FarmController.FindChestWithSpecificSeed used `Inventory.GetItem(seedPrefabName, -1, false)`, which matches m_shared.m_name ($item_...), never the prefab name. Replaced that one call with `MHR_SeedRules.GetItemByPrefab(inv, prefab, quality, worst)` (same stack shape; prefab match, falls back to GetItem). UpdateTakingSeeds already used m_shared.m_name correctly. Plantable dump: Acorn, AncientSeed, Barley, BeechSeeds, BirchSeeds, Carrot, CarrotSeeds, FirCone, FirConeFrost, Flax, KaleSeeds, MushroomJotunPuffs, MushroomMagecap, OatSeeds, Onion, OnionSeeds, PineCone, PoteitrSeeds, Sap, Seed_*_RtD x14, Turnip, TurnipSeeds, VineberrySeeds, VineGreenSeeds.
- Tool trips (same chest twice): approach spot (-266.1, 40.1, -308.3); Pathfinding path always ended at (-264.4, 39.9, -307.6) (GetPath requireFullPath=true still returned true with a short path); companion hovered 1.3-2.5m from the chest, LineClear false, gave up after 20s. User: door at top of the stairs is almost always open; chests are in tight spaces. Jumps fired while he was walking fine around the building (distance-to-goal metric).
- Fixes: LineClear ignores colliders under any Container and colliders whose bounds' largest side < 1.2m. PickApproach uses GetPath(requireFullPath=false, cleanup=true, partial=true) and only accepts a spot whose path end is within 0.6m (horizontal) and 1.0m (vertical) of it. New `MHR_Chests.CanReach(me, chest)` (within 2.5m + LineClear, else PickApproach; cached 30s per chest). ToolKeeper.FindBestInChests now takes the companion position, sorts candidates by score and takes the first reachable ("Can't reach the chest at (..) - trying another"; none reachable -> best anyway + log). Returns: original chest only if reachable, else FindChestWithRoom prefers reachable (falls back to nearest). MHR_Nav stuck = no 1m movement in 3s OR no 0.5m closer in 8s; log shows "no closer for Ns" / "standing still Ns".
- PlantAny default now "CarrotSeeds, TurnipSeeds, OnionSeeds, Barley, Flax, MushroomJotunPuffs, MushroomMagecap, VineberrySeeds"; Resolve also accepts a unique suffix match (JotunPuffs -> MushroomJotunPuffs). Existing configs keep their old value.

Previous version: **1.4.66** — build tag `v84-chests-paths-seeds`. Verified: branches 0/1798, maxstack 0/1798, check_refs 0, ResolveRefs 17714/17714, JIT 1738/1738, version 1.4.66. Untested in-game.
- User log 15058c0e (1.4.65, Community Patch OFF; LogOutput.log only keeps the last launch): every plant scan "Nothing to plant" although chests had carrots/turnips. Root cause: all our helper chest searches used Physics.OverlapSphereNonAlloc with a 256-collider buffer; FindRipePickable reported 512 colliders in 30m at this base, so chests were often never returned. FarmController.ScanNearbyChests uses Piece.GetAllPiecesInRadius (no cap) and found all 24. Also JotunPuffs/Magecap reported not plantable (unknown why - new Debug dump of s_seedToPlant keys at first use). User runs a farming mod adding Seed_*_RtD items (Jotunn). Tool trips completed but took the cultivator through a wall (approach point on the straight line chest->companion, arrival = within 2.5m). User saw him fail at the stairs: walks along the right side of them, back and forth.
- New `MHR_Chests` (in MHR_ToolKeeper.cs): `Near(center, r)` = Piece.GetAllPiecesInRadius + GetComponentInChildren<Container>, deduped. Used by ToolKeeper (FindBestInChests/FindChestByPos/FindChestWithRoom), SeedRules.CountNear, GatherRules stock count; GatherRules.NearBuildings also uses GetAllPiecesInRadius. `LineClear(from, chest)`: RaycastAll from from+1m to chest+0.5m on Default/static_solid/Default_small/piece/terrain/vehicle, ignoring the chest's own colliders and Characters. `PickApproach(me, chest, out spot)`: 8 directions x radius 1.2 then 1.7, floor by raycast down, |dy| <= 1.2, 0.35m headroom sphere at +1m clear, LineClear, scored by Pathfinding.GetPath(Humanoid, requireFullPath) length (no path = 1000+straight). ToolKeeper: PickSpot at trip start and every 4s while travelling; opens the chest when within 1m of the spot, or within 2.5m with LineClear; otherwise keeps moving (no more through-wall grabs).
- New `MHR_Nav.Move(ai, dt, p, d, run, tag)` / `MoveFarm` (FarmController's 4 MoveToPoint calls replaced 1:1): wraps CompanionAI.MoveToPoint; per-AI progress = distance to goal improving by 0.5m (reset when the goal moves >1.5m). Debug every 2s: `[Path|Farm/Tools] at (..) -> (..) Xm left, path=yes (n corners, next (..))/NO, no progress for Ns`. Stuck 3s, on ground: face the next path corner >1m away (else the goal) rotated 0/+35/-35/0 degrees, Character.Jump(false), set horizontal rb velocity 3.5 m/s that way; 1.5s between tries, 4 tries per goal; logs "stuck at .. - jumping toward .. (try n)".
- SeedRules: seeds of a two-step crop are eligible if > SeedMinimum OR (> 0 and no vegetables of that crop) - bootstrap. "Nothing to plant" Info line lists "CarrotSeeds 8/20, Carrot 0; ...". PlantAny names resolve case-insensitively.

- User log fb9bd387 (1.4.64): set farm zones -> "running all over": Farm was walking to a seed chest while Stay Home was on; HomesteadController (chores) only yields to Harvest/Repair/Rest, so it repaired a woodpole and headed for a chair mid-trip. Turning Farm off started a cultivator return trip; user picked Follow; trip (MoveToPoint from Plugin Update) and AI movement fought -> slow stutter, distance grew 9.6 -> 18.7m. Any zone picked BirchSeeds (tree seeds) because no carrot/turnip seeds existed.
- **Jobs vs Stay Home (user: mutually exclusive):** new C# `MHR_JobRules` (in MHR_ToolKeeper.cs): `OnJobSet(cs, mode)` turns StayHome off when mode != 0 - called in CompanionRadialMenu.SetActionMode (after ZDO set, with the new mode V_2) and point-command DirectSmelt (mode 6) / DirectGatherMode. ToggleStayHome turning on with a job -> SetActionMode(0). `HasJob(cs)` gates HomesteadController.IsHomesteadMode (false while a job is on, covers old saves). Radial label "Stay Home" -> "Homestead" (loc default hc_radial_stayhome); bed config text updated.
- **Tool trips (user: always finish unless fighting; passive keeps going):** ToolKeeper.Tick (Plugin Update) still decides and does the chest transfer, but movement moved to `MHR_ToolKeeper.Drive(ai, dt)` called in CompanionAI.UpdateAI right after UpdateTorch (combat already handled above it): busy -> clear follow target, MoveToPoint(goal, 0.6, run) or StopMoving, return true (follow/patrol/job dispatch skipped). In combat the trip pauses (TripStart shifted, progress clock reset) instead of giving up. Removed "job changed mid-fetch -> stop": a fetch finishes even if the job changed (then the return logic puts it back). HomesteadController.ShouldAbort and CompanionRest.TryAutoSleep return early while IsBusy. EndTrip still ApplyFollowMode(mode, true) so Follow/new job take over after.
- **Farm planting (user design):** MHR_SeedRules rewritten. Config Farm: SeedReserve field now bound as key `SeedMinimum` (20, 0-1000), new `SeedTarget` (100, 0-5000), `PlantGroupSize` (4, 1-100), `PlantAny` ("CarrotSeeds, TurnipSeeds, OnionSeeds, Barley, Flax, JotunPuffs, Magecap, VineberrySeeds"; unknown names warned once). Families: seed S + source X (X = seed-source whose grown Pickable drops S), or self-replanting S. `PickSeed(farm, zoneCrop)` (zoneCrop "" = Any): slots in list order S1,X1,S2,...; S eligible if stock(S) > min (self-replanting: > 0), X eligible if stock(S) < target and stock(X) > 0; stock = pack + chests within 30m of home (10s cache, cleared on plant/store). Keeps current slot until PlantGroupSize planted (`OnPlanted` hook after RemoveItemByPrefab in PlantSeed), then next eligible slot. Specific zones: same rules for that one family; a zone set to the source (Carrot) plants it as asked. TryScanPlant: after V_10 (isAny), V_9 = PickSeed(...); null -> "[Plant] Zone[n] nothing to plant here right now" and next zone; else V_10 = false so everything goes through FindSpecificSeedInInventory / FindChestWithSpecificSeed.
- **Storage:** IsCropItem (now `assembly`) uses `WantsStore(farm, item)`: storable crop, or a plantable with pack total > SeedMinimum. UpdateStoringOutput's loop replaced by `StoreOutput(farm, pack, chest)`: crops moved whole; plantables only the amount over SeedMinimum (Clone+AddItem, RemoveItem(it, n)).
- Not changed: cultivated-ground check (user to A/B Community Patch); farm chest approach point.

Previous version: **1.4.64** — build tag `v82-gather-rules`. Verified: branches 0/1766, maxstack 0/1766, check_refs 0, ResolveRefs 17548/17548, JIT 1708/1708, version 1.4.64. Untested in-game.

- **Gather rules (user design, Part A of the wood-job plan):** C# `MHR_GatherRules` (MHR_GatherRules.cs). Config, Harvest section: WoodLimit/StoneLimit/OreLimit (int 500, 0-100000, 0 = none); GatherWood "Wood, FineWood, RoundLog, ElderBark, YggdrasilWood, Blackwood, Resin", GatherStone "Stone, Flint, BlackMarble, Grausten", GatherOre "CopperOre, TinOre, IronScrap, SilverOre, BlackMetalScrap, CopperScrap, FlametalOreNew, Obsidian" (entries Name or Name:N; blank N = category default, 0 = unlimited; unlisted = skip; names case-insensitive; unknown names logged once as a warning vs ObjectDB); WoodSkipTrees "Oak" (substring of standing TreeBase name); BaseProtectRadius 10 (float 0-50; player-built Piece on piece/piece_nonsolid layers within r of a standing tree -> skip; cached 30s per tree); JunkCleanup true; NeverDiscard "Trophy".
- Hooks: UpdateIdle's ScanForTarget call -> `MHR_GatherRules.Scan(h, mode)` (counts limit vs other rejections; null result with limit rejections -> "[Gather] ... at its limit - waiting" + speech hc_speech_plenty_wood/stone/ore, 120s throttle). ScanForTarget: after the candidate null check, `AllowTarget(h, go, mode)` (modes 1-3 only): skip list + base protect for TreeBase, then the target's drops (TreeBase m_dropWhenDestroyed + m_logPrefab->TreeLog chain via m_subLogPrefab; TreeLog; MineRock/MineRock5 m_dropItems; DropOnDestroyed) must include a listed item under its limit (stock = own inventory + non-companion/non-tombstone chests within 40m of home/self, cached 10s per item); no known drops -> allowed. ScanForDrops: `WantDrop` (modes 1-3: drop must be on that mode's list) before the overweight check; when CanAddItem fails, `TryMakeRoom`: home + HarvestController.FindNearestChest(home) -> sets PackFull so IsOverweight() reports true (existing deposit trip; flag clears once a slot frees); else if JunkCleanup -> ItemDrop.DropItem junk (Material/Trophy/Misc, no food value, not on any gather list, not a seed key, not NeverDiscard, not equipped) with m_autoPickup=false, RemoveItem. IsOverweight = weight >= threshold || PackFull. Update: after the pause gate, `TickDodge` (2.5s MoveToPoint to the dodge point, cancelled in combat). UpdateAttacking "Target DESTROYED": `OnTargetDestroyed(h, _targetType)` for TreeBase/TreeLog: dodge 4m perpendicular to companion forward, picking the higher-ground side (ZoneSystem.GetGroundHeight). UpdateMoving: the MoveToPoint point goes through `ApproachPoint` (trees/logs: 8 samples at 2.5m; if a side is >0.4m higher, offset 0.8m toward it, fixed per target). Made HarvestController._target, ScanForTarget, FindNearestChest `assembly`.
- Side effect: Ore mode no longer takes MineRock5 that only drop Stone (agent report: Ore accepted every MineRock5).
- Part B (piles) agreed but not built: UsePiles toggle; shared pile zones (editor like farm zones; zone per material or Any); placement priority zone > same-material piles > same-group piles > clear flat spot near home away from buildings; needs a hammer (ToolKeeper); fall back to chest when no room. Confirm stack capacities/pieces in-game first. User also wants later: ForageLimit/FoodLimit style lists; auto farm zones from hoed ground.

Previous version: **1.4.62** — build tag `v80-job-tools`. Verified: branches 0/1736, maxstack 0/1736, check_refs 0, ResolveRefs 17296/17296, JIT 1679/1679, version 1.4.62. Untested in-game.
- **Tool fetch/return (user design):** C# `MHR_ToolKeeper` (source MHR_ToolKeeper.cs in work/, stubs + spliced class body, same technique as MHR_Diag). Ticked every frame from CompanionsPlugin.Update (after MHR_RespawnTracker.Tick). Per owned companion: mode = ZDO ActionMode. Needs: 1 Wood=axe (m_chop>0, weapon/tool type, no buildPieces), 2/3 Stone/Ore=pickaxe (m_pickaxe>0), 7 Hunt=bow (ItemType.Bow) + ammo matching bow m_ammoType, 8 Farm=cultivator (buildPieces containing a Plant piece), 9 Fish=rod (AnimationState.FishingRod) + bait (ammo matching rod), 15 Repair buildings=hammer (buildPieces name contains "Hammer"). Missing -> best item (toolTier*10000 + quality*1000 + dmg) in usable chests within 30m of home (or itself): Container not on a Character/TombStone/Vagon/Ship, not in use, valid ZNetView, Private chests only if Piece creator == local player. Trip: SetFollowTarget(null), CompanionAI.MoveToPoint each frame until within 2.5m, 40s timeout; take via Inventory.MoveItemToThis(chestInv,item); record borrowed {kind,prefab,count,chestPos} in ZDO string "HC_Borrowed" ("kind|prefab|count|x|y|z;..."). Return when the new mode doesn't need that kind: to the same chest (1.5m search at saved pos) else nearest chest with room; ammo partial via Clone+AddItem/RemoveItem. After a trip: `CompanionSetup.ApplyFollowMode(mode, true)`. Nothing found -> 60s cooldown, log, speech for axe/pickaxe/arrows only (new loc keys hc_speech_need_axe/_pickaxe/_arrows; other jobs already speak). Skips while sleeping/navigating to bed or in combat. While a trip is running: `MHR_ShouldPauseWork` returns true (Harvest/Smelt/Farm/Fish/Cook/Repair pause) and the CompanionSetup follow watchdog (the "Follow target was null - restored" block, old IL_03a7) returns early. Hunt/RepairBuild/Restock controllers aren't gated (they idle without their tool). Decided NOT to auto-stop the job when no tool exists (it just says so, every 60s) - told the user.
- **Seed reserve (user: default 20, adjustable):** C# `MHR_SeedRules` (MHR_SeedRules.cs). Config Farm > SeedReserve (int 20, 0-200). "Seed source" = item in s_seedToPlant whose plant prefab name contains "seed" and whose grown Pickable drops a different item that is itself a planting key (Carrot -> sapling_seedcarrot -> CarrotSeeds). AllowPlant(farm,item): seed sources only while count of the seeds (own inventory + non-companion chests within 30m of home/self, cached 10s) < reserve; logs "[Farm] Seed reserve: n/20 ..." (60s throttle). Hooked in FindPlantableSeedInInventory (after the cultivator-seeds Contains) and FindChestWithSeeds. IsCropItem now uses IsStorableCrop(name): not a planting key -> crop; planting key -> crop only if it's a seed source (vegetables stored, seeds and self-replanting barley/flax kept). Previously every planting key (incl. vegetables) was kept and replanted as seed plants indefinitely.

Previous version: **1.4.61** — build tag `v79-ui-wake-farm-forage-fixes`. Verified: branches 0/1700, maxstack 0/1700, check_refs 0, ResolveRefs 17021/17021, JIT 1645/1645, version 1.4.61. Untested in-game. (User log 7e93a81c, 1.4.60.)
- **Chest overlay root cause:** CompanionInteractPanel.BuildUI clones `InventoryGui.m_container` (the vanilla chest panel) once; any chest item element GameObjects under the grid's `m_gridRoot` came along and were never managed (clone's m_elements is a fresh list). New static `MHR_ClearCopiedIcons(grid)` DestroyImmediate's every child of `_grid.m_gridRoot`, called at BuildUI's old IL_00e7 (right after _grid is found); logs "[UI] Cleared N leftover chest icon(s)".
- **Wake shove:** vanilla Player.AttachStop puts the player at `m_attachPoint.TransformPoint(m_detachOffset)` (bed: attach = Bed.m_spawnPoint, detachOffset (0,0.5,0)) BEFORE re-enabling collision. New `CompanionRest.MHR_StandUpFromBed(bed)` (only if _isSleeping) does the same (spawnPoint or bed transform), zeroes Rigidbody velocity, syncs rb position; called in StopSleeping before MHR_SetBedCollisionIgnored(bed,false).
- **Self-restart:** NotifyControllersInventoryChanged now calls `FarmController.MHR_NotifyIfIdle()` (only if _phase <= Scanning) and `HarvestController.MHR_NotifyIfIdle()` (only if _state == Idle) instead of NotifyActionModeChanged unconditionally (same bug class as the 1.4.40 smelt fix).
- **Farm drops:** crop Pickables (single harvest) are destroyed right after Interact, so UpdateHarvesting saw `_targetPickable == null` and went straight to Scanning, skipping CollectingDrops. Now: target null and `_actionTimer != 0` (already interacted) -> phase CollectingDrops with 0.5s wait (PickUpOneDrop uses own position when target null).
- **IsCropItem** was "any Material-type item that isn't a seed" (stored wood/resin/meat/leather/dandelions). Now: not equipped, not a seed (s_seedToPlant), and drop prefab name in `s_cropOutputNames`.
- **Forage skips field crops:** forage scan uses new `HarvestController.MHR_ForageAllowed(p)` = IsForageItemAllowed && not (name starts with Pickable_Turnip/Carrot/Onion/Barley/Flax, or MHR_SafeCultivated(pos)). NOTE: log showed cultivated=False for every planted turnip - cultivated check may not work (VCP?); name list covers vanilla crops regardless. Watch planting (needs it).
- **Doors in jobs:** UpdatePassingThrough: if _passTimer > 3 and no follow target (job driving movement) -> ResetToIdle(true) leaving the door open, log "Door is open - handing movement back to the job". HarvestController.UpdateMoving skips the 10s move-timeout/blacklist while `DoorHandler.IsActive` (new `MHR_DoorBusy`).
- **Diag:** MHR_Diag IL skips companions whose ZDO ActionMode != 0.
- Restock checked: Restock and Homestead refuel use each Fireplace's own `m_fuelItem` via exact prefab-name match (FindItemByPrefab) - no FineWood/CoreWood possible for vanilla fires. Asked user if they saw otherwise (no answer yet).

Previous version: **1.4.60** — build tag `v78-delete-guard`. Verified: branches 0/1694, maxstack 0/1694, check_refs 0, ResolveRefs 16987/16987, JIT 1639/1639, version 1.4.60. Untested in-game.
- **Delete guard (user decision: general protection + toggle, keep the SKK marker too):** Harmony prefix `MHR_ZNetSceneDestroy_Patch` on `ZNetScene.Destroy(GameObject go)` (TargetMethod via AccessTools; ZNetView.Destroy() funnels into it). `MHR_DeleteGuard.Allow(go)` returns true (let it through) unless: config `General > ProtectCompanions` (bool, default true) is on AND go has CompanionSetup AND not `_mhrDespawning` AND not `_mhrDying` AND Character health > 0. Blocked -> returns false + throttled (5s) LogWarning "[Protect] Blocked another mod from deleting companion ... Called from: <stack trace, 600 chars>". Prefix wrapped in try/catch -> allow on any error.
- Legit paths checked: game `Character.OnDeath` invokes m_onDeath (our OnCompanionDeath now sets new `_mhrDying` first) before ZNetScene.Destroy, health <= 0 anyway; our Despawn (MHR_DespawnConfirmed -> DespawnWithTombstone now sets `_mhrDespawning` as its first instruction, then ZNetView.Destroy). Our other ZNetScene.Destroy calls are tombstones/items, not companions. Zone unload / remote ZDO removal use Object.Destroy directly (not hooked). CompanionAI extends BaseAI (not MonsterAI) so MoveAwayAndDespawn never runs for companions. `_mhrDespawning` and new `_mhrDying` are now `assembly` visibility. Direct `ZDOMan.DestroyZDO` by a mod would bypass the guard (rare).

Previous version: **1.4.59** — build tag `v77-ward-mod-compat`. Verified: branches 0/1691, maxstack 0/1691, check_refs 0, ResolveRefs 16968/16968, JIT 1636/1636, version 1.4.59. Untested in-game.
- **SKKmods GuardStonesRemoveMonsters compat:** user supplied the DLL. Its Harmony postfix on `BaseAI.UpdateAI` returns early if the creature's ZDO has bool `"SKK_NoDestroy"` (string-key `ZDO.GetBool(string,bool)`); otherwise, inside an enabled player-faction ward, it skips players, bosses, Hildir bosses, event creatures (unless RemoveRaidEvent), "trainingdummy" names, anything with a `Tameable` component or hatchling/piggy/chicken/calf/cub names (unless RemoveTameable), then sets `SKK_RequestDestroy` and calls `ZNetView.Destroy()` (owner) or RPC_RequestDestroy. It also sets SKK_NoDestroy itself for the exempt cases. Fix: new `CompanionSetup.MHR_MarkNoDestroy()` (owner-only, once per instance via `_mhrNoDestroyDone`) sets `ZDO.Set("SKK_NoDestroy", true)`; called from CompanionSetup.Awake (right after _nview is cached) and Update (after MHR_StampOwnerDeathRule). No dependency on the mod; harmless without it.

Previous version: **1.4.58** — build tag `v76-job-logging-farm-fix`. Verified: branches 0/1690, maxstack 0/1690, check_refs 0, ResolveRefs 16963/16963, JIT 1635/1635, version 1.4.58. Untested in-game.
- **Job-by-job pass started (user decision):** same process as smelting (1.4.36-1.4.40): turn on logging, user tests one job, fix what the log shows, repeat. Order: Farm, Fish, Gather (wood/stone/ore), Hunt, Cook, Homestead. User OK with adjusting behavior along the way.
- **Logging was compiled out** in ProfMags' build for FarmController (Log, LogDebug), FishController (Log) and HarvestController (Log, LogInfo): 1-byte `ret` stubs while 63/21/59 call sites existed. Bodies now mirror SmeltController::Log: `[Farm|name]` Log=LogInfo, LogDebug=LogDebug; `[Fish|name]` LogInfo; `[Gather|name]` Log=LogDebug, LogInfo=LogInfo. LogMovement stubs left off (noisy). FindRipePickable logs every collider per scan - trim if too noisy.
- **Farm crash (user log 2a2d9595, 1.4.57):** 2202x `InvalidProgramException: Invalid IL code in FarmController:FindRipePickable (): IL_011a: stloc.s 11` (the `Heightmap` local from `Heightmap.FindHeightmap`). Passes desktop-Mono JIT sweep + ResolveRefs; user runs ValheimCommunityPatch (applies "HeightmapLookupPatch"), suspected cause (Unity Mono stloc type-compat check vs. patched/inlined callee). Fix: new `FarmController.MHR_CultivatedRaw(Vector3)` (NoInlining, no typed local: FindHeightmap -> dup/brtrue -> IsCultivated) wrapped by `MHR_SafeCultivated` (try/catch; on any exception sets `s_mhrCultBroken`, one LogWarning, returns false thereafter). All 3 FindHeightmap sites (FindRipePickable, FindPlantPosition, FindPlantPositionInZone) use it. Not confirmed whether VCP is the cause - user may test with it disabled.
- **Food log spam:** CompanionFood.ClampHealth logged "[Food] ClampHealth — x → x (food expired?)" every frame when health was a hair over max (108k of 133k lines). Still clamps every time; now only logs when over by > 0.5.
- **Ward disappearance report (not ours):** player uses SKKmods "Guardstone wards remove monsters" (Nexus 3132): destroys (ZNetScene destroy, not kill) any AI character inside an active ward except bosses, training dummies and optionally tameables (RemoveTameable default false). Companions count as AI characters -> destroyed, no tombstone. Need its DLL to see how it picks targets before writing a compat exclusion.
